# Agents · R · part 42

970 entries, rule-test-author to runtime-watcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **rule-test-author**: Writes or repairs the firing TEST MATERIAL for an EXISTING generated rule — the negative-test / bad-good samples the deterministic factory needs to prove a rule actually fires — then runs the lane's deterministic verification in single-rule isolation and quotes the tool verdict verbatim. Edits test material ONLY; never the emitted rule artifact (that is drift/hash-gated). Use when a consumer has a generated rule whose test material is missing, broken, or needs a bypass variant, and wants it verified without re-running the full research pass. NOT for creating new rules — that is /rule-research… ([artyhoo/getff](https://github.com/artyhoo/getff/blob/HEAD/agents/rule-test-author.md) · ★1)

- **rules**: 1. Output is a claim, not knowledge. 2. A claim is proven only by: a source fetched this session, a file path and line read this session, or output of a deterministic command run this session. 3. Anything else is unproven and is labeled unproven. 4. Recalled facts, citations, numbers and quotes are unproven. 5. Confidence, fluency and self-reports are not proof. 6. - **rules**: 1. **`from __future__ import annotations`** — first line of every Python file, no exceptions 2. **Type hints on every function** — parameters + return type, always 3. **No `print()` calls** — use `loguru` via `from core.logger import get_logger` 4. **No bare `except:`** — always catch specific exceptions 5. **No hardcoded secrets** — all API keys via `.env` + `python-dotenv` 6. **No blocking I/O** ([Fujel-Patel/Voice-Assistent-vs-code](https://github.com/Fujel-Patel/Voice-Assistent-vs-code/blob/HEAD/agents/rules.md))

- **RUNBOOK**: 1. Read the rules: `ravi specs get cli/agents --mode rules --json`. 2. Reproduce the failing call with `--json` and read `error.code` first; the code, not the message, is the branch point. 3. Exit `1` + `AGENT_NOT_FOUND`: read `error.suggestions` — real agent ids and names similar to what was asked. Retry with one of them, or list with `ravi agents list --json`. 4. - **runtime-runnability-review**: 代码可运行性审查 + 代码质量 + 运行效率/算法复杂度优化角色。MUST BE USED after any code modification in this project (CODEBUDDY.md §4.1). 专抓"编译/单测全绿但真实运行路径会崩"的盲区，并负责性能优化（先测试保功能不变再优化）。 ([heiferleaf/Auto-Test-Generation](https://github.com/heiferleaf/Auto-Test-Generation/blob/HEAD/.codebuddy/agents/runtime-runnability-review.md))
- **runtime-safety**: Reviews runtime safety on two axes — concurrency (goroutine lifecycle and leaks, object ownership, shared mutable state, races, context propagation, bounded buffers and timeouts) and secret hygiene (passwords or WS-Security digests reaching stdout, a log line, or a URL). Use proactively on any diff that adds a goroutine, a wg.Go, a shared field, a log call, or a struct field that can hold a credential. - **runtime-safety**: You are a paranoid security agent specializing in **runtime safety, denial of service vectors, race conditions, prototype pollution, ReDoS, and memory safety issues**. - **runtime-safety-auditor**: Audits a Hono/Node service for the runtime-safety pitfalls that bleed data or crash under load — module-scope `new Map()`/Fluid-Compute cross-request state (S-01/P-02), SSE traps (S-08/P-10/P-11/P-12), the Zod `.default()`-on-PATCH data-loss trap (S-02/P-04/P-05), and stale/vulnerable version pins (S-11). Use BEFORE shipping a generated or hand-written service. Reports findings keyed by P-/S-ID with file:line + the fix. Distinct from contract-reviewer (which owns OpenAPI/RPC contract drift, not runtime safety).
